- **optimize-prefill-vs-decode-kernels**: 2. **Compute arithmetic intensity for each dominant matmul.** For an M×K @ K×N matmul: FLOPs ≈ `2*M*K*N`, bytes (fp16) ≈ `(M*K + K*N + M*N) * 2`, intensity = FLOPs / Bytes. - Prefill, M=2048, K=N=8192, fp16: ~1300 FLOPs/byte. - Decode, M=1, K=N=8192, fp16: ~1 FLOP/byte (dominated by weight load). - Spec-decode, M=8, K=N=8192, fp16: ~8 FLOPs/byte — still memory-bound, but closer to the knee.
